`fast_b(a)`:
```
def fast_b(a):
    n = a.size
    size = 2*n -1
    m = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A*A, m)[:size]
    # numerical fix
    b[np.abs(b)<1e-15]=0? 
    return b
```
But repeated rfft of a each iteration. In mirror update, we need b for max and p, and h. h can use p and a. If p changes. We can compute h via rfft of rev(a) and p. `conv_len = n+len(p)-1 = 3n-2? Wait p length 2n-1, a_rev length n => conv length 3n-2. Previous code used fft_size next pow2 conv_len, Ar=rfft(a_rev), Pr=rfft(p), conv=irfft(Ar*Pr), grad=conv[n-1:2n-1]. Is that correct? We need h_i = ∑_{k=0}^{2n-2} p_k a_{k-i} (0≤k-i<n). Define a_rev? Let r_j = a_{n-1-j}. Convolution r * p: c_m =∑_j r_j p_{m-j}. We want for i: ∑_k p_k a_{k-i}. Let j=n-1-i, then a_{k-i}=r_{?} r_j = a_{n-1-j}. Need k-i = n-1-j => k = i+n-1-j. Then ∑_k p_k a_{k-i}=∑_j p_{i+n-1-j} r_j. That's convolution p * r at index i+n-1? (p * r)_m =∑_j p_{m-j} r_j. Set m=i+n-1 => p_{i+n-1-j}. Yes. So slice m=n-1 to 2n-2 length n. Good.

If p is normalized sum=1, h_i approx derivative /2. For objective max, subgradient at peak k is 2 a_{k-i}. If p uniform over top peaks, h as above.

Update:
```
def mirror_step(a, p, gamma):
    h = cross_conv_kernel(p, a)  # length n
    # center h to reduce global scale
    mu = h.mean()
    # use scaled = (h-mu)/std
    sd = h.std()
    if sd < 1e-15: return a
    z = h - mu
    # ensure nonnegative? update a*exp(-gamma*z/scale)
    a_new = a * np.exp(-gamma * z / scale)
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0); sum; normalize
```
If gamma too large, underflow. Use scale = std(h) or max-min. If gamma 1, changes by e^- std. For normalized differences, good. If gamma up to 20, can aggressively move. Need line search: try gamma values (0.2,0.5,1,2,4,8,16) and accept if M decreases. But evaluating M for each gamma requires fast_b. Could accept first improvement; or best. To speed, maybe use limiting step: gamma = step_size * (M? no). Use adaptive: start with gamma=1, if no improvement, try 0.5, 2, 0.2, etc. Use time.

But exponential update may keep a smooth if a had zeros? Add jas. Also objective may require moving mass from high h coordinates to low h; exp(-gamma h) plus normalization does that.

Implement gaussian basis:
```
def make_gauss_basis(n, R):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    centers=np.linspace(0,1,R)
    sigma=0.15
    Phi=np.empty((n,R))
    for r in range(R):
        d=x-centers[r]; Phi[:,r]=np.exp(-d*d/(2*sigma*sigma))
    # maybe normalize each basis to unit norm? Not necessary but scale of θ matters.
    # Remove identity? constant dropped by softmax.
```
Bases all positive; softmax with positive bases can't create zeros but okay. The feature dimension 30. Could use multiple sigma (multi-scale sigma 0.05,0.15,0.4). R total maybe 30.

Objective for low-dim:
```
def lowdim_eval(theta, basis, n, beta, mode='logsum'):
    z = basis.dot(theta) # n
    # stability: z -= max
    z -= z.max()
    F = np.exp(z)
    s = F.sum()
    if s <= 0: return inf, None
    a = F/s
    b = fast_b(a)
    L = len(b)
    # logsumexp
    bu = b - b.max()
    expb = np.exp(bu/beta)?? Wait logsumexp(b/beta) = beta log sum exp((b-bmax)/beta) + bmax. For gradient p=exp((b-bmax)/beta)/sum.
    q = (b - bmax)/beta; clip; expq; p=expq/sum; value = bmax + beta*log(sumexpq)
    # objective J = 2n value? Since sum=1. return value maybe.
    # grad:
    G_i = 2*h where h = cross_conv_kernel(p,a)
    v = G_i * a
    first = basis.T @ v
    total = v.sum()
    mean = basis.T @ a
    grad = first - total*mean
    return 2n*value, grad
```
Need `cross_conv_kernel(p,a)` function. p length 2n-1; a length n. p sum 1. For bmax etc.

Beta: Need choose relative to b values. b avg ~1/(2n). Set `beta = rel * b.mean()` maybe. For smooth start rel=20 (very smooth); decrease to 0.05 (sharp). But `logsumexp(b)` with b ~1/n: if β = bmean * rel, p near uniform for rel large. Good.

Minimize value maybe `score = 2n * value`, but scale by 2n constant. Use value to avoid large.

L-BFGS-B may benefit from scaling theta. Initialize theta=0. For analytic starts, set z = log(target density), solve θ approx? Hard. Maybe use mirror as main, it might outperform FISTA. Let's design robust `mirror_search(a, time_limit, ...)` returning best. It can schedule beta rel and gamma. Pseudocode:
```
def mirror_search(a0, budget, beta_rel_start=5.0, beta_rel_end=0.05, gamma0=1.0, top_mode='smooth', verbose):
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_m=fast_b(a).max()
    b=fast_b(a); it=0; stall=0; rng=...
    t0=time
    beta_rel = beta_rel_start
    gamma=gamma0
    while time and stall<stallmax:
       # every N iters reduce beta, reset gamma?
       p=make_active(b, beta_rel)
       h=cross_kernel(p,a)
       # choose gamma via backtracking
       # If p changes, h scale. Normalize h deviations:
       dev = h - h.mean()
       sd = dev.std()
       if sd <= 1e-18: gamma_use=0
       for cand in [gamma, gamma*0.5, gamma*2, gamma*0.25, gamma*0.2]:
           a_new = a * exp(-cand * dev/sd) # if sd large
           normalize, clip
           m = fast_b(a_new).max()
           if m < best_m - tol: update, accept, break
       # If no accept, reduce gamma or beta, perturbation, stall
       if accept: a=a_new; b=fast_b_new; stall=0
       else: stall++; if stall>...: random restart or beta change
```
But evaluating multiple candidates per iter costs multiple FFTs. We can choose gamma fixed and accept if improvement over current (not best) or if Armijo. Maybe simpler: Use gamma=step (e.g., 1) and if new max < current max, accept; else try 0.5, 0.25, 0.1. This is line search. Each iteration maybe 3 FFTs. However `fast_b` also for b and m. We need recomputing p requires b. For accepted a_new, reuse b_new. For try candidates, can't all reuse. We can use objective only (max b) via fast_b.

Maybe too slow for many restarts. We can use fewer candidates: one step with adaptive gamma from previous.

Use update `a_new = a * exp(-gamma * (h - h_mean)/scale)`. Since normalization divides, adding constant to h doesn't matter. `scale` should be chosen so gamma in O(1-10). `fast_b(a)`:
Need stable and fast. We can cache FFT size? Maybe function computes. For repeated n same, overhead small. Could create `fft_size_for_n(n)` function. Use `a` as contiguous float64.
```
def fast_b(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2*n -1
    m = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:size]
    # For nonnegative exact, clip tiny negatives
    if b.size:
        b[b < 0] = 0.0 # But if negative? True nonnegative, numerical. Clipping changes gradient maybe okay.
    return b
```
But if a not normalized, sum b = sum^2. For max and gradient, if we normalize a each time, OK. Clipping can create discontinuities but tiny.

`cross_kernel(p,a)`:
```
def cross_kernel(p,a):
    n=a.size
    rev = a[::-1]
    conv_len = len(p)+n-1
    m = 1 << (conv_len -1).bit_length()
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(rev, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Ar * Pr, m)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```
`p` length 2n-1, sum 1. If p all zero? return zeros.

`norm_sum1(a)`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[a<0]=0
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

`smooth_active(b, beta_rel, rng=None, top_quantile=None)`:
```
def active_weights(b, beta_rel, rng=None, subset=0.0):
    bmax=b.max()
    meanb=b.sum()/len(b)
    beta=beta_rel*meanb
    if beta<=0: beta=meanb
    q=(b-bmax)/beta
    # clip
    q=np.where(q < -50.0, -700.0, q)
    p=np.exp(q)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.zeros_like(p); idx=np.argmax(b); p[idx]=1
    else: p/=s
    # optional restrict to top quantile? Could multiply by mask where q > -20? 
```
For sharp beta, exp(-50) ~1.9e-22, ok. Inf? q max0, exp 1. Use q=-700 underflows to 0. OK.

Potential underflow if beta very small relative b; q clamp -700. Use `np.exp(np.clip(q, -745, 0))`.

b=fast_b(a); return 2*len(a)*b.max()/(a.sum()**2)

Let's refine mirror_search algorithm.

Pseudo:
```
def mirror_search(a0, budget, beta_rel_start=10.0, beta_rel_min=0.05, gamma_start=1.0, max_stall=200, verbose_label=''):
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    if a.size < 2: return a, inf
    b=fast_b(a); M=b.max(); best=a.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic()
    gamma=gamma_start
    beta_rel=beta_rel_start
    stall=0
    restart=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    it=0
    n=len(a)
    # maybe fixed ladder of beta_rel
    # Each phase reduce beta after some no improvement
    while time < budget:
        # if time phase? every 100 iters if no improvement reduce beta_rel? Maybe use schedule based on it:
        beta_rel = max(beta_rel_min, beta_rel_start * 0.995**it) # too slow? 0.995^1000=0.61.
        p=active_weights(b, beta_rel, rng)
        h=cross_kernel(p,a)
        dev=h-h.mean()
        scale=dev.std()
        if scale < 1e-18:
            # no gradient; perturb
        candidates = [gamma, gamma*0.5, gamma*2, gamma*0.25, gamma*0.1]
        # order: try medium first
        accepted=False
        best_trial=None
        for g in candidates:
            if time > budget: break
            z = -g * (dev / scale)
            # clip to avoid overflow; max dev/scale ~ few
            z=np.clip(z, -30, 30)
            an = a * np.exp(z)
            an = np.maximum(an, 0)
            an += 1e-300? maybe
            sn=an.sum()
            if sn<=0: continue
            an=an/sn
            bn=fast_b(an); mn=bn.max()
            if mn < M - 1e-15:
                a=an; b=bn; M=mn; gamma=g; accepted=True; break
            # keep if improves best? maybe
            if best_trial is None or mn < best_trial[1]: best_trial=(an,bn,mn,g)
        if accepted:
            if M < best_M - tol: best=a.copy(); best_M=M
            stall=0
        else:
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial[2] < best_M - 1e-15:
                a=best_trial[0]; b=best_trial[1]; M=best_trial[2]; gamma=best_trial[3]; accepted=True; stall=0; update best
            else:
                stall +=1
                gamma *= 0.85
                if gamma < 0.01: gamma=gamma_start; break to restart? 
        if stall > max_stall:
             # restart from best with noise or beta thr change
             noise=0.001*(1+restart*0.2)
             a = best + rng.normal(0, noise, n)*best? use absolute noise
             a=np.maximum(a, 1e-12); a=a/a.sum()
             b=fast_b(a); M=b.max()
             beta_rel=max(beta_rel_min, beta_rel_start*(0.7+0.1*restart))
             gamma=gamma_start
             stall=0; restart+=1
        it+=1
        if verbose and it%200==0: print
```
Need avoid creating an each candidate floor huge. Use `an = a * exp` if a some zeros remain zero; normalization. To allow movement into zeros, add tiny epsilon to a before update: `a_pos = a + 1e-16` maybe but normalization changes. Could do in update: `base = a + 1e-18` then normalize? That adds total n*1e-18 negligible. Use `base = a.copy(); base += 1e-18?` For n=8000, 8e-15 relative. OK. However for extremely small positive values, can matter. Use `base = np.maximum(a, 1e-20) + 1e-20`; normalize base before update? Or just multiply `a` with zeros stays zero; but if zero positions have low h, we might want grow. Add small floor. We can maintain positivity by after normalization: `an[an < 1e-30] = 1e-30`. Sum negligible. Use `an = np.maximum(an, 1e-300)`? Underflow if underflows to0; then floor. Good.

Step size: dev/scale std=1. gamma candidates [1,0.5,2,0.25,0.1]. But if update too strong, may worsen. Good.

Beta schedule: We can change every e.g. 500 iters if no severe stall:
```
if it % 300 == 0: beta_rel *=0.7
```
Also when restart.

Use `M` current. For active p, if beta_rel low and M small, p normalized. We also need p length matches b. Good.

Does mirror descent actually decrease max? It reduces coordinates with high h. Suppose a uniform, b triangular max center. h for center peak = a(mid) roughly same everywhere? Gradient for central peak k~n/2: h_i≈a_{k-i}, which is high near i≈k/2? It may reduce center coordinates, moving mass to edges, maybe lowers central peak? Usually yes. For edge peaks, h near edges.

Potential issue: Exponential update normalizes and can create new high b. Line search only accepts improvement; may be slow but safe. def fft_next(m):

`fast_b` use contiguous:
```
def fast_b(a):
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.zeros(0)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:size]
    # remove negative tiny
    b = np.where(b < 0.0, 0.0, b)
    return b
```
Clipping all negatives may be O(n), fine. But if a has zeros and numerical negative small, okay. If significant negative due to overflow? no.

`cross_kernel` with p maybe normalized:
```
def cross_kernel(p,a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return p.copy()
    rev = np.ascontiguousarray(a[::-1], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.ascontiguousarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    conv_len = len(p)+n-1
    m=1<<(conv_len-1).bit_length()
    Pr=np.fft.rfft(p,m); Ar=np.fft.rfft(rev,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Ar*Pr,m)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check orientation: For p weights length L=2n-1. We want h_i = ∑_k p_k a_{k-i} for k-i in [0,n-1]. Using conv Ar*Pr where Ar=FFT(a_rev), Pr=FFT(p). Let's test with small n manually: a=[a0,a1], p length3 [p0,p1,p2]. rev=[a1,a0]. conv c length4: c0=a1p0; c1=a1p1+a0p0; c2=a1p2+a0p1; c3=a0p2. Slice n-1=1 to 2n-1=3 length2: [c1,c2] = [a1p1+a0p0, a1p2+a0p1]. We want for i=0: h0 = p0 a0 + p1 a1 + p2 a2? only k-i in [0,1] => k=0: p0a0; k=1:p1a1. But slice gives c1=a0p0+a1p1 yes. i=1: h1=p1 a0 + p2 a1 = c2 yes. Good. Note p0 coefficient shifted? For active peak k=0, derivative wrt a1? None because k-i negative. Our h1 doesn't include p0, correct. Good.

`norm_sum1_safe` as above, with floor? For optimization, floor maybe `eps=1e-300`? We can return exactly sum. To avoid zero sum:
```
def normalize_positive(a, floor=1e-30):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a<0]=0.0
    a += floor
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=1e-15:
       a=np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    else: a/=s
    return a
```
But adding floor and normalizing changes best slightly; okay. For best return, maybe no floor? To satisfy nonnegative, floor tiny okay. But if floor=1e-30, no effect. Use floor=0 for evaluation? def score_internal(a):

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64)
    old_n=f.size
    s=f.sum()
    if old_n==new_n: return f.copy()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    f=f/s
    if old_n==1: return np.full(new_n,1.0/new_n)
    old_x=np.linspace(0,1,old_n,endpoint=True)
    new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n,endpoint=True)
    g=np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    g=np.maximum(g,0)
    gs=g.sum()
    if gs<=0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    return g/gs
```
Endpoint true means shape endpoints preserved. Good.

`try_shift`:
```
def evaluate_rolled(a, shift):
    ar=np.roll(a, -shift)
    m=fast_b(ar).max()
    return ar, m
```

       shifts=np.arange(n)
    else:
       shifts=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0], rng.integers(0,n,n_eval))))

    # local refine around best: for delta in range(-step,step)

Mirroring search function: Need avoid global variables? Use rng. It can call print. We need know time. Implementation details:
```
def mirror_search(a0, budget, label='mirror', beta_rel_start=8.0, beta_rel_end=0.02, eta_start=0.3, max_stall=150, verbose=True):
    a=normalize_positive(a0, floor=0)
    n=a.size
    b=fast_b(a); M=b.max(); best_score=2*n*M; best_a=a.copy()
    if not finite or budget<=0: return...
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(987654)
    eta=eta_start
    beta_rel=beta_rel_start
    use_log = True
    stall=0; iter=0
    while time.monotonic()-t_start < budget:
       # occasionally shift best
       if iter>0 and iter%250==0:
          # sample up to 8 shifts of best_a
          for _ in range(8):
             s=int(rng.integers(0,n))
             ar,m=evaluate_rolled(best_a,s)
             sc=2*n*m
             if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_a=ar.copy()
       p=active_weights(b, beta_rel, cut=20.0)
       h=cross_kernel(p,a)
       h_mean=np.mean(h); 
       dev_lin=h-h_mean; std=dev_lin.std()
       # update candidates
       accepted=False; best_cand=None
       candidates=[]
       if use_log:
          h_safe=np.maximum(h, 1e-30)
          h_safe_mean=np.mean(h_safe)
          if h_safe_mean>0:
             z_base=-np.log(h_safe/h_safe_mean) # this is +(log mean - log h)
             for e in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*2.0, eta*0.25]:
                 candidates.append((z_base*e, e, 'log'))
       else:
          if std>1e-18:
             z_base=-dev_lin/std
             for g in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*2.0, eta*0.25]:
                 candidates.append((z_base*g, g, 'lin'))
       # ensure some candidate even if no
       if not candidates:
           # random small perturbation
           ...
       for z,g,type in candidates:
           z=np.clip(z, -30, 30)
           an = a * np.exp(z)
           an = np.maximum(an, 1e-30)
           s=an.sum()
           if s<=0: continue
           an/=s
           bn=fast_b(an); mn=bn.max()
           if mn < M - 1e-15:
               a=an; b=bn; M=mn; accepted=True
               if 2*n*M < best_score: best_score=...
               break
           # if best_cand improve current? but not <M maybe.
           if best_cand is None or mn < best_cand[1]: best_cand=(an,bn,mn,g)
       if accepted:
           stall=0
           # if g smaller? adjust eta? If accepted last g? We can set eta maybe same.
       else:
           # if best_cand improves best_score (but not current) we might accept even if worse than current to escape? Could be risky.
           if best_cand is not None and 2*n*best_cand[1] < best_score - 1e-12:
               a=best_cand[0]; b=best_cand[1]; M=best_cand[1]; stall=0; update best
           else:
              stall+=1
              eta *= 0.8
              if eta < 0.02:
                  eta=eta_start
                  # perturb if many stall
       if stall >= max_stall:
           # restart from best with noise and random shift
           noise_scale = 0.02 / n * (1 + 0.2*min(stall//max_stall,5))
           ar=best_a + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n)
           # maybe roll
           if rng.random()<0.3: ar=np.roll(ar, int(rng.integers(0,n)))
           a=normalize_positive(ar)
           b=fast_b(a); M=b.max()
           if 2*n*M < best_score: best_score=...
           beta_rel = max(beta_rel_end, 0.7*beta_rel)
           eta=eta_start
           use_log = not use_log
           stall=0
       # schedule: every 150 iters reduce beta_rel and flip? Reducing maybe if no progress.
       if iter>0 and iter%200==0:
           beta_rel=max(beta_rel_end, beta_rel*0.85)
       use_log = not use_log? If flip every iter maybe too noisy; use alternate type within candidates? Maybe use_log = (iter%2==0).
       iter+=1
       if verbose and iter%500==0: print
    return best_a, best_score
```

This can drastically speed final phases. Let's implement `cross_kernel_adaptive(p,a, threshold=256)`.
```
def cross_kernel_adaptive(p,a):
    n=a.size
    # count nonzeros with tolerance
    if p.size == 0: return zeros
    maxp=p.max()
    if maxp==0: return zeros
    tol=1e-14
    nz=np.flatnonzero(p > maxp*1e-12)
    if len(nz) < 256:
       h=np.zeros(n)
       for k in nz:
          pk=p[k]
          i0=max(0,int(k)-n+1); i1=min(n-1,int(k))
          if i0<=i1:
             j0=int(k)-i0; j1=int(k)-i1
             h[i0:i1+1] += pk * a[j1:j0+1][::-1]
       return h
    else:
       rev=a[::-1]; conv_len=p.size+n-1; m=...; return irfft(...)
```
Need k length p up to 2n-2. `a[j1:j0+1]` length; if j1 > j0? Should not: i0<=i1 => j0=k-i0 >= k-i1=j1. Good. Slice `a[j1:j0+1]` returns j1..j0, reverse gives j0..j1? Wait we need a[j0], a[j0-1],...,a[j1]. `a[j1:j0+1]` is [a[j1],...,a[j0]], reverse [a[j0],...,a[j1]], correct. Good.

Let's design local pair search with direct pair delta using fast_b only.
```
def local_pair_search(a0, budget, label='local'):
   a=normalize_positive(a0); n; b=fast_b; M=b.max(); best=...; t0; rng
   stall=0
   while time-budget and stall<200:
      b=fast_b(a); M=b.max(); top_peaks=np.argsort(b)[::-1][:12]
      peak_weights = np.zeros(len(p)); peak_weights[top_peaks]=1/len
      h = cross_kernel_adaptive(peak_weights,a)
      # sources: top contributing to each peak
      sources=[]
      for k in top_peaks[:8]:
         i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
         if i0<=i1:
            idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1)
            prod=a[idx]*a[k-idx]
            local=np.argsort(prod)[::-1][:2]
            sources.extend(int(idx[t]) for t in local)
      # dests: low h
      low=np.argsort(h)[:min(20,n//10+1)]
      # also random low
      # try pairs
      improved=False
      for s in unique sources[:8]:
         if a[s] <=1e-15: continue
         for d in unique low[:8]:
            if d==s: continue
            for frac in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7]:
               amt=a[s]*frac
               an=a.copy(); an[s]-=amt; an[d]+=amt
               # if an min negative? no
               # normalize? sum same; but to fix floor: an=clean
               an[an<0]=0; an /= an.sum()
               bn=fast_b(an); mn=bn.max()
               if mn < M - 1e-15:
                   a=an; b=bn; M=mn; improved=True; stall=0; if score<best update; break all? break frac
            if improved: break
         if improved: break
      if not improved:
         # random perturb maybe similar to mirror; or stall++
         stall+=1
         if stall>50: random small moves 10
         if stall>200: break
      # print
   return best_a,best_score
```

def lowdim_search(n, budget, parent_seq=None, max_targets=10):
    t0; basis=make_basis(n)
    # targets list densities length n
    targets=[]
    if parent_seq is not None and len(parent_seq)==n: targets.append(parent_seq)
    else if parent_seq valid: targets.append(interpolate_sequence(parent_seq,n))
    # other analytic targets
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for p in [0.5,1,2]: targets.append(sinc pow)
    ... limit total maybe max_targets+3
    max_targets_total=12
    starts=[]
    for target in targets[:max_targets_total-3]:
       z=np.log(target+1e-300); z-=z.mean(); theta=np.linalg.lstsq(basis,z,rcond=None)[0]; starts.append(theta)
    # random
    rng=np.random.default_rng(2)
    for _ in range(3): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.5,basis.shape[1]))
    best_theta=None; best_a=...; best_score=inf
    for idx(theta) in enumerate(starts):
       if time > budget*0.85 break
       theta, a, sc = optimize_start(theta,basis,n, [5.0,1.0,0.2], maxiter=35)
       if sc<best_score: update
    return best_a,best_score
```

def initial_candidates(n, include_parent=None):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    arrs=[]
    arrs.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for p in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0]: arrs.append(norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi*x)**p))
    for p in [0.5,1.0,2.0]: arrs.append(norm_sum1(np.maximum(1-x,1e-30)**p))
    for p in [0.5,1.0]: arrs.append(norm_sum1(np.maximum(x,1e-30)**p))
    for amp in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
       a=1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*x); arrs.append(norm_sum1(np.maximum(a,1e-30)))
       a=1.0-amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*x); ...
       a=1.0+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x); ...
    for c in [0.25,0.5,0.75]:
       for sigma in [0.1,0.25]: arrs.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(-((x-c)/sigma)**2)))
    if include_parent is not None: arrs.append(norm_sum1(include_parent))
    return arrs
```

Let's implement `active_weights` top-level:
```
def active_weights(b, beta_rel, cut=20.0):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L=b.size
    if L==0: return b.copy()
    bmax=float(b.max())
    s=float(b.sum())
    meanb = s/L if s>0 else 1e-12
    beta = beta_rel*meanb
    if beta <= 1e-300: beta = meanb if meanb>0 else 1e-12
    q=(b-bmax)/beta
    q=np.clip(q, -745.0, 0.0)
    if cut is not None and cut > 0:
        q=np.where(q < -cut, -745.0, q)
    p=np.exp(q)
    ps=p.sum()
    if ps <=0:
       p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
    else: p = p/ps
    return p
```
In q where q<-cut, set -745. If cut 20, weights below 2e-9 cut. Good.

`logsumexp_weights` similar but without cut? Could use cut=None.

In lowdim, `p, smooth = logsumexp_weights(b, beta)`; return p and value. Implement:
```
def logsumexp_info(b,beta):
    bmax=b.max(); q=(b-bmax)/beta; q=np.clip(q,-745,0); expq=np.exp(q); s=expq.sum(); p=expq/s if s>0 else onehot; val=bmax+beta*np.log(s); return p,val
```
For beta small, q top 0, rest -745. OK.

`lowdim_fgp(theta, basis, base_log, n, beta_rel)`:
```
    z = basis.dot(theta)
    if base_log is not None: z = z + base_log
    zmax=z.max()
    if not np.isfinite(zmax): return inf, zeros
    z = z - zmax
    F=np.exp(z)
    s=F.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return inf, zeros
    a=F/s
    b=fast_b(a)
    meanb=b.sum()/b.size; beta=beta_rel*meanb if meanb>0 else 1e-12
    p, smooth = logsumexp_info(b, beta)
    h=cross_kernel_adaptive(p,a)
    G=2.0*h
    v=G*a
    first=basis.T.dot(v)
    total=v.sum()
    mean=basis.T.dot(a)
    grad=first - total*mean
    # clean grad
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    score = 2*n*smooth
    return score, grad
```
`base_log` may be None. If basis shape n x R. If n != basis.shape[0] but passed, okay.

def lowdim_start(theta0, basis, base_log, beta_list, maxiter):
    theta=theta0.copy()
    a=theta_to_a(theta,basis,base_log)
    best_score=score_internal(a)
    best_a=a.copy()
    R=basis.shape[1]
    for beta in beta_list:
       try:
          res = minimize(lowdim_fgp, theta, args=(basis, base_log, a.size, beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=...)
       except Exception: break
       if res.x is not None and finite:
          theta=res.x.copy()
          a=theta_to_a(theta,basis,base_log)
          sc=score_internal(a)
          if sc<best_score: update
       else: break
    return theta,best_a,best_score

def local_pair_search(a0,budget,label='local'):
   a=normalize_positive(a0); n=len; b=fast_b; M=b.max(); best_score=2*n*M; best_a=a.copy(); t0; rng=default_rng
   stall=0; outer=0
   while time-budget and stall<150:
      b=fast_b(a); M=b.max(); sc=2*n*M
      if sc<best_score: update
      top_peaks=np.argsort(b)[::-1][:10]
      pw=np.zeros(len(b)); pw[top_peaks]=1.0/len(top_peaks)
      h=cross_kernel_adaptive(pw,a)
      sources=[]
      for k in top_peaks[:6]:
          k=int(k)
          i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
          if i0>i1: continue
          idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1,dtype=np.int64)
          prod=a[idx]*a[k-idx]
          # choose top 2
          if prod.size <=2: order=np.argsort(prod)[::-1]
          else: order=np.argpartition(prod, -2)[::-1]? Need top 2. Use argsort for small? prod length up to n; argpartition faster:
              part_idx = np.argpartition(prod, -2)[-2:]
              order = part_idx[np.argsort(prod[part_idx])[::-1]]
          for ii in order[:2]: sources.append(int(idx[ii]))
      low_candidates=np.argsort(h)[:min(20,max(5,n//100))]
      # add a few random excluding sources?
      if n > 50:
         rnd = rng.choice(n, size=min(10,n), replace=False)
         for rr in rnd: low_candidates=np.append(low_candidates, rr)
      # make unique low candidates
      low_candidates=np.unique(low_candidates)
      # build pairs source-dest not same and a[source]>eps
      pairs=[]
      for s in np.unique(sources):
          if a[s] < 1e-12: continue
          for d in low_candidates:
              if d != s:
                  pairs.append((int(s), int(d)))
                  if len(pairs) > 80: break
          if len(pairs)>80: break
      improved=False
      for (s,d) in pairs:
          # maybe if d source? skip
          for frac in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8):
              if time budget: break
              amt=a[s]*frac
              an=a.copy(); an[s]-=amt; an[d]+=amt
              an[an<0]=0; ssum=an.sum(); if ssum<=0: continue; an/=ssum
              bn=fast_b(an); mn=bn.max()
              if mn < M - 1e-15:
                  a=an; b=bn; M=mn; sc=2*n*M; best? improved=True; break
          if improved: break
          # optionally if evaluated all pairs? enough
      if improved:
          stall=0
          if 2*n*M < best_score: best_score=...; best_a=a.copy()
      else:
          stall +=1
          # if stall small, try random 3 moves not requiring source list? or just continue with new active?
          if stall % 20 == 0:
             for _ in range(5): random frac? maybe skip for time
          if stall > 80:
             # use mirror-ish small restart? break and let other algorithms
             break
      outer+=1
      if verbose and outer%100==0: print
   return best_a,best_score
```

Now, detail of `make_basis`: Standardize columns. If a column std zero, set zeros. Avoid adding constant. Code:
```
def make_basis(n, use=True):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cols=[]
    for k in range(1,5): cols.append(np.cos(2πkx)); cols.append(np.sin(...))
    cols.append(x)
    cols.append(x*x - 0.5?) # after standardize no matter
    cols.append(x*x*x)
    for c in [0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9]:
       for sig in [0.06,0.15,0.3]:
          d=x-c; cols.append(np.exp(-d*d/(2*sig*sig)))
    basis=np.column_stack(cols)
    # standardize
    means=basis.mean(axis=0)
    stds=basis.std(axis=0)
    stds[stds<1e-12]=1
    basis=basis-means
    basis=basis/np.sqrt(2.0*stds*stds) # /std? sqrt(2)*std gives variance 0.5; okay. Use /stds.
    basis=basis - basis.mean(axis=0) # recenter maybe.
    return np.ascontiguousarray(basis)
```
After standardize, some columns near constant? We centered Fourier already mean zero. Gaussian mean not zero, subtract. Good.

Dimension 8+3+15=26. If n small, okay.

def shift_search(a0,budget=10.0,n_eval=128,seed=1):
    a=normalize_positive(a0); n=a.size; t0; b=fast_b(a); best_m=b.max(); best_shift=0
    if n<=1 or budget<=0: return a,2*n*best_m
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if n_eval >= n and n <= 4096:
       shifts=np.arange(n,dtype=int)
    else:
       shifts=np.empty(0,int); shifts=np.concatenate(([0], rng.integers(0,n,n_eval)))
    # unique not necessary
    for s in shifts:
       if time > budget: break
       ar,m=evaluate_rolled(a,int(s)%n)
       if m<best_m: best_m=m; best_shift=int(s)%n
    # local refine
    candidates=[]
    for d in range(-20,21): candidates.append((best_shift+d)%n)
    # also if n_eval large? random around best
    for d in candidates:
       if time > budget: break
       ar,m=...
       if m<best_m: update
    ar=np.roll(a,-best_shift)
    return ar,2*n*best_m
```

def consider(best_seq,best_score,a,label,t0):
    if a is None: return best_seq,best_score
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return
    a[~np.isfinite(a)]=0; a[a<0]=0; a[a>1000]=1000
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return
    sc=score_internal(a)  # uses sum^2
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy()
       print
    return best_seq,best_score
```

Detailed function `normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    a /= s
    return a
```
No floor. For log base, use floor separately.

`make_base_log`: `a=normalize_positive(a); return np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))`.

```
def score_internal(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return float('inf')
    a=a.copy(); a[~finite]=0; a[a<0]=0; a[a>1000]=1000
    s=a.sum(); if s<0.01 return inf
    m=fast_b(a).max()
    return 2*a.size*m/(s*s)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The implementation uses:
  - FFT-based exact self-convolution evaluation,
  - low-dimensional smooth parameterization (log-softmax over a fixed basis),
  - active-set mirror/exponential descent on the full sequence,
  - targeted two-coordinate local moves,
  - cyclic shift exploration.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def fast_b(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a via FFT, with tiny negatives clipped to zero."""
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:size]
    if b.size:
        np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def score_internal(a):
    """Objective, with the same clipping used by the external evaluator."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    a[a > 1000.0] = 1000.0
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    m = fast_b(a).max()
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def normalize_positive(a):
    """Normalize to sum 1, keeping finite nonnegative entries."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    a /= s
    return a


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    """Normalize and linearly interpolate one sequence to another length."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n = f.size
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if old_n == new_n:
        return normalize_positive(f)

    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    f = f / s

    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, endpoint=True)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, endpoint=True)
    g = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    gs = g.sum()
    if gs <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return g / gs


def cross_kernel_adaptive(p, a, direct_threshold=96):
    """
    h_i = sum_k p_k * a_{k-i}, valid range only.

    For sparse active sets p, use direct shifted accumulation;
    otherwise use FFT convolution.
    """
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.ascontiguousarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    maxp = float(p.max())
    if maxp <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    nz = np.flatnonzero(p > maxp * 1e-12)
    if nz.size <= direct_threshold:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in nz:
            k = int(kk)
            pk = float(p[k])
            if pk == 0.0:
                continue
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i0 > i1:
                continue
            j0 = k - i0
            j1 = k - i1
            h[i0:i1 + 1] += pk * a[j1:j0 + 1][::-1]
        return h

    rev = np.ascontiguousarray(a[::-1], dtype=np.float64)
    conv_len = p.size + n - 1
    m = 1 << (conv_len - 1).bit_length()
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(rev, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Ar * Pr, m)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def active_weights(b, beta_rel, cut=20.0):
    """
    Softmax-over-convolution-coefficients weights, optionally cut off.
    beta_rel is relative to mean(b).
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = b.size
    if L == 0:
        return b.copy()

    bmax = float(b.max())
    s = float(b.sum())
    meanb = s / L if s > 0.0 else 1e-12
    beta = beta_rel * meanb
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        beta = meanb if meanb > 0.0 else 1e-12

    q = (b - bmax) / beta
    q = np.clip(q, -745.0, 0.0)
    if cut is not None and cut > 0.0:
        q = np.where(q < -cut, -745.0, q)
    p = np.exp(q)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = p / ps
    return p


def logsumexp_info(b, beta):
    """Return p_k proportional to exp(b_k/beta), normalized, and smooth max."""
    bmax = float(b.max())
    q = (b - bmax) / beta
    q = np.clip(q, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(q)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return p, bmax
    p = e / s
    return p, bmax + beta * np.log(s)


def make_basis(n):
    """
    Fixed basis for smooth low-dimensional parameterization.
    Columns are centered by the softmax convention.
    """
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    cols = []

    for k in range(1, 5):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))

    cols.append(x)
    cols.append(x * x)
    cols.append(x * x * x)

    for c in (0.10, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90):
        for sig in (0.06, 0.15, 0.30):
            d = x - c
            cols.append(np.exp(-(d * d) / (2.0 * sig * sig)))

    basis = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    means = basis.mean(axis=0)
    stds = basis.std(axis=0)
    bad = stds < 1e-12
    stds[bad] = 1.0
    basis = (basis - means) / stds
    basis -= basis.mean(axis=0)
    basis[~np.isfinite(basis)] = 0.0
    return np.ascontiguousarray(basis)


def make_base_log(a):
    """Log baseline for local multiplicative/softmax parameterization."""
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))


def theta_to_a(theta, basis, base_log=None):
    z = np.asarray(basis, dtype=np.float64).dot(theta)
    if base_log is not None:
        z = z + np.asarray(base_log, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(z).all():
        return normalize_positive(z)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        return normalize_positive(z)
    F = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(F.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(basis.shape[0], dtype=np.float64) / basis.shape[0]
    return F / s


def lowdim_fgp(theta, basis, base_log, beta_rel):
    """
    Objective and gradient for a log-softmax parameterization.
    Parameter theta controls z = base_log + basis @ theta.
    """
    n = basis.shape[0]
    R = basis.shape[1]
    z = basis.dot(theta)
    if base_log is not None:
        z = z + base_log

    if z.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(z).all():
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)

    zmax = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)

    F = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(F.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)

    a = F / s
    b = fast_b(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)

    bsum = float(b.sum())
    meanb = bsum / b.size if bsum > 0.0 else 1e-12
    beta = beta_rel * meanb
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        beta = meanb if meanb > 0.0 else 1e-12

    p, smooth = logsumexp_info(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(smooth):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)

    h = cross_kernel_adaptive(p, a)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

    G = 2.0 * h
    v = G * a
    first = basis.T.dot(v)
    total = float(v.sum())
    mean = basis.T.dot(a)
    grad = first - total * mean
    grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0

    return float(2.0 * n * smooth), grad


def optimize_lowdim_start(theta0, basis, base_log, beta_list, maxiter):
    """Run L-BFGS-B on a small smooth max objective for several betas."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if theta.size != basis.shape[1]:
        theta = np.zeros(basis.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)

    a = theta_to_a(theta, basis, base_log)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_internal(a)

    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return theta, best_a, best_score

    for beta_rel in beta_list:
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                lowdim_fgp,
                theta,
                args=(basis, base_log, beta_rel),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxls": 12,
                    "disp": False,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is not None:
            x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
            if x.size == theta.size and np.isfinite(x).all():
                theta = x.copy()
                a = theta_to_a(theta, basis, base_log)
                sc = score_internal(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            break

    return theta, best_a, best_score


def top_k_indices(arr, k):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0 or k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    k = min(k, n)
    idx = np.argpartition(arr, -k)[-k:]
    return idx[np.argsort(arr[idx])[::-1]]


def bottom_k_indices(arr, k):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0 or k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    k = min(k, n)
    idx = np.argpartition(arr, k)[:k]
    return idx[np.argsort(arr[idx])]


def evaluate_rolled(a, shift):
    ar = np.roll(a, -int(shift))
    m = float(fast_b(ar).max())
    return ar, m


def shift_search(a0, budget=10.0, n_eval=128, seed=101):
    """Find a better cyclic roll of the zero-padded sequence."""
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or budget <= 0.0:
        return a, 2.0 * n * float(fast_b(a).max())

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    b0 = fast_b(a)
    best_m = float(b0.max())
    best_shift = 0
    best_a = a.copy()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if n <= 2048 or n_eval >= n:
        shifts = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    else:
        shifts = rng.integers(0, n, size=int(n_eval), dtype=int)
        shifts = np.concatenate((np.array([0], dtype=int), shifts))

    for s in shifts:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
            break
        ar, m = evaluate_rolled(a, int(s) % n)
        if m < best_m - 1e-18:
            best_m = m
            best_shift = int(s) % n
            best_a = ar.copy()

    # Local refinement around the best roll.
    candidates = []
    for d in range(-24, 25):
        candidates.append((best_shift + d) % n)
    if n > 100:
        extra = rng.integers(0, n, size=24, dtype=int)
        for e in extra:
            candidates.append(int(e))

    for s in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
            break
        ar, m = evaluate_rolled(a, int(s) % n)
        if m < best_m - 1e-18:
            best_m = m
            best_shift = int(s) % n
            best_a = ar.copy()

    best_a = np.roll(a, -best_shift)
    return best_a, 2.0 * n * float(best_b_max := fast_b(best_a).max())


def initial_candidates(n, parent_seq=None):
    """A dictionary of standard smooth nonnegative starting shapes."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    arrs = []

    if parent_seq is not None:
        pp = interpolate_sequence(parent_seq, n)
        arrs.append(pp)

    arrs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n))

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** p))
        arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-12) ** p))
        arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.maximum(x, 1e-12) ** p))

    for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
        arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-12)))
        arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.maximum(1.0 - amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-12)))
        arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-12)))

    for c in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75):
        for sig in (0.08, 0.20, 0.35):
            d = x - c
            arrs.append(normalize_positive(np.exp(-(d * d) / (2.0 * sig * sig))))

    return arrs


def build_target_starts(n, basis, parent_seq, max_targets=10, seed=202):
    """Convert standard target densities into initial theta vectors."""
    targets = initial_candidates(n, parent_seq)
    targets = targets[:max(1, max_targets)]
    starts = []
    R = basis.shape[1]

    for target in targets:
        t = normalize_positive(target)
        if t.size != n:
            t = interpolate_sequence(t, n)
        if t.sum() <= 1e-15:
            continue
        z = np.log(np.maximum(t, 1e-60))
        z = z - float(z.mean())
        try:
            theta = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, z, rcond=None)[0]
        except Exception:
            theta = np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)
        theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        theta[~np.isfinite(theta)] = 0.0
        starts.append(theta)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for _ in range(2):
        th = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, size=R)
        starts.append(th)

    return starts


def lowdim_search(n, basis, parent_seq, budget):
    """Low-dimensional smooth search around parent and from standard starts."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    best_a = normalize_positive(parent_seq) if parent_seq is not None else None
    best_score = score_internal(best_a) if best_a is not None else float("inf")

    if budget <= 10.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    # Time-adaptive schedules.
    if budget > 180.0 and n <= 9000:
        beta_local = [6.0, 2.0, 0.6, 0.18]
        maxit_local = 45
        beta_global = [5.0, 1.5, 0.45, 0.12]
        maxit_global = 35
        n_targets = 9
    elif budget > 80.0:
        beta_local = [4.0, 1.0, 0.25]
        maxit_local = 30
        beta_global = [3.0, 0.7, 0.18]
        maxit_global = 25
        n_targets = 6
    else:
        beta_local = [2.0, 0.5]
        maxit_local = 18
        beta_global = [1.5, 0.35]
        maxit_global = 14
        n_targets = 3

    parent_n = interpolate_sequence(parent_seq, n) if parent_seq is not None else None

    # Local parameterization around the current parent.
    if parent_n is not None:
        base_log = make_base_log(parent_n)
        zero = np.zeros(basis.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)

        for th0 in (zero, np.random.default_rng(11).normal(0.0, 0.2, size=basis.shape[1])):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > 0.50 * budget:
                break
            _, a_try, sc_try = optimize_lowdim_start(th0, basis, base_log, beta_local, maxit_local)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                best_score = sc_try
                best_a = a_try.copy()
                print("    lowdim local parent: " + repr(round(float(sc_try), 7)))

    if time.monotonic() - t0 < 0.85 * budget:
        starts = build_target_starts(n, basis, parent_n, max_targets=n_targets)
        for th0 in starts:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > 0.90 * budget:
                break
            _, a_try, sc_try = optimize_lowdim_start(th0, basis, None, beta_global, maxit_global)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_score:
                best_score = sc_try
                best_a = a_try.copy()
                print("    lowdim global start: " + repr(round(float(sc_try), 7)))

    return best_a, best_score


def mirror_search(a0, budget, label="mirror", beta_rel_start=8.0, beta_rel_end=0.02,
                  eta_start=0.7, verbose=True):
    """
    Full length mirror/exponential descent with active-set weights.

    Updates a_i proportional to a_i * exp(-eta * centered_gradient)
    and performs cautious line search by accepting only true max reductions.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or budget <= 0.0:
        b = fast_b(a)
        return a, 2.0 * n * float(b.max())

    b = fast_b(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    best_score = 2.0 * n * M
    best_a = a.copy()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(55507 + (n % 9973))
    eta = float(eta_start)
    beta_rel = float(beta_rel_start)
    stall = 0
    restarts = 0
    use_log = True
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
        # If current point drifted far behind best, jump back to best.
        if 2.0 * n * M > best_score + 2e-5:
            a = best_a.copy()
            b = fast_b(a)
            M = float(b.max())

        # Periodically try rolls of the best sequence.
        if it > 0 and it % 160 == 0:
            for _ in range(6):
                s = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                ar, mr = evaluate_rolled(best_a, s)
                if 2.0 * n * mr < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best_score = 2.0 * n * mr
                    best_a = ar.copy()

        p = active_weights(b, beta_rel, cut=20.0)
        h = cross_kernel_adaptive(p, a)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

        if use_log:
            hmean = float(h.mean())
            if hmean <= 1e-300:
                zbase = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                hs = np.maximum(h, 1e-30)
                ratio = hs / max(hmean, 1e-300)
                zbase = -np.log(ratio)
        else:
            hm = float(h.mean())
            sd = float(h.std())
            if sd <= 1e-18:
                zbase = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                zbase = -(h - hm) / sd

        zbase = np.clip(zbase, -5.0, 5.0)
        if not np.isfinite(zbase).all():
            zbase = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        accepted = False
        accepted_eff = eta
        factors = (1.0, 2.0, 0.5)
        best_trial = None

        for f in factors:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
                break
            eff = max(0.02, min(5.0, eta * f))
            zuse = np.clip(eff * zbase, -50.0, 50.0)
            an = a * np.exp(zuse)
            an = np.maximum(an, 1e-30)
            s = float(an.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            an /= s

            bn = fast_b(an)
            mn = float(bn.max())
            trial_score = 2.0 * n * mn

            if best_trial is None or trial_score < best_trial[2]:
                best_trial = (an, bn, trial_score, mn)

            if mn < M - 1e-15:
                a = an
                b = bn
                M = mn
                accepted = True
                accepted_eff = eff
                if trial_score < best_score:
                    best_score = trial_score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break

        if accepted:
            eta = accepted_eff
            stall = 0
            if verbose and it % 700 == 0:
                el = time.monotonic() - t0
                print("    " + label + " it=" + str(it) + " t=" + repr(round(el, 1)) +
                      " score=" + repr(round(float(best_score), 7)) +
                      " beta_rel=" + repr(round(float(beta_rel), 3)) +
                      " eta=" + repr(round(float(eta), 3)))

        else:
            # Accept a line-search trial if it improves the global incumbent,
            # even if it is not better than the current point.
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial[2] < best_score - 1e-14:
                a = best_trial[0]
                b = best_trial[1]
                M = best_trial[3]
                best_score = best_trial[2]
                best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                eta = max(0.05, min(5.0, eta * 0.95))
            else:
                stall += 1
                eta *= 0.85
                if eta < 0.04:
                    eta = float(eta_start)
                    use_log = not use_log

                if stall % 45 == 0:
                    beta_rel = max(float(beta_rel_end), beta_rel * 0.72)

                if stall > 260:
                    # Restart from best with multiplicative noise and possible shift.
                    noise_scale = 0.08 * (1.0 + 0.08 * min(restarts, 25))
                    ar = best_a * (1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, size=n))
                    if rng.random() < 0.35:
                        ar = np.roll(ar, int(rng.integers(0, n)))
                    a = normalize_positive(ar)
                    b = fast_b(a)
                    M = float(b.max())
                    if 2.0 * n * M < best_score:
                        best_score = 2.0 * n * M
                        best_a = a.copy()

                    beta_rel = min(float(beta_rel_start), max(float(beta_rel_end), beta_rel * 1.25))
                    eta = float(eta_start)
                    use_log = not use_log
                    stall = 0
                    restarts += 1
                    if verbose:
                        el = time.monotonic() - t0
                        print("    " + label + " restart #" + str(restarts) +
                              " t=" + repr(round(el, 1)) +
                              " incumbent=" + repr(round(float(best_score), 7)))

        use_log = not use_log
        it += 1

        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
            break

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def local_pair_search(a0, budget, label="local", verbose=True):
    """
    Targeted two-coordinate mass transfers chosen from active peaks.
    Full FFT evaluation is used for each candidate move.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2 or budget <= 0.0:
        b = fast_b(a)
        return a, 2.0 * n * float(b.max())

    b = fast_b(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    best_score = 2.0 * n * M
    best_a = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42424)
    stall = 0
    outer = 0

    max_trials_per_outer = 55

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget and stall < 150:
        b = fast_b(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        sc = 2.0 * n * M
        if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        top_peaks = top_k_indices(b, min(10, b.size))
        pw = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if top_peaks.size > 0:
            pw[top_peaks] = 1.0 / float(top_peaks.size)

        h = cross_kernel_adaptive(pw, a)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)

        sources = []
        for kk in top_peaks[:6]:
            k = int(kk)
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i0 > i1:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            prod = a[idx] * a[k - idx]
            prod = np.where(np.isfinite(prod), prod, 0.0)
            if prod.max() <= 1e-30:
                continue
            loc = top_k_indices(prod, min(2, prod.size))
            for j in loc:
                sources.append(int(idx[j]))

        if len(sources) == 0:
            sources = [int(x) for x in rng.integers(0, n, size=min(5, n))]

        low_count = max(8, min(30, n // 50))
        low_idx = bottom_k_indices(h, low_count)
        if n > 50:
            extra = rng.integers(0, n, size=min(12, n), dtype=int)
            low_idx = np.concatenate((low_idx, extra))
        low_idx = np.unique(low_idx.astype(int))

        sources = np.unique(np.asarray(sources, dtype=int))
        pairs = []
        for s_idx in sources:
            si = int(s_idx)
            if si < 0 or si >= n or a[si] <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for d_idx in low_idx:
                di = int(d_idx)
                if di < 0 or di >= n or di == si:
                    continue
                pairs.append((si, di))
                if len(pairs) >= max_trials_per_outer:
                    break
            if len(pairs) >= max_trials_per_outer:
                break

        if len(pairs) == 0:
            pairs = [(int(x), int(y)) for x, y in
                     zip(rng.integers(0, n, size=20), rng.integers(0, n, size=20))
                     if x != y and a[x] > 1e-12][:max_trials_per_outer]

        improved = False
        fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.70)

        for si, di in pairs:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
                break
            if si < 0 or si >= n or di < 0 or di >= n or si == di:
                continue
            if a[si] <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for fr in fracs:
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
                    break
                amt = a[si] * float(fr)
                an = a.copy()
                an[si] -= amt
                an[di] += amt
                an[an < 0.0] = 0.0
                ss = float(an.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                an /= ss
                bn = fast_b(an)
                mn = float(bn.max())
                if mn < M - 1e-15:
                    a = an
                    b = bn
                    M = mn
                    sc = 2.0 * n * mn
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if verbose and outer % 60 == 0:
                el = time.monotonic() - t0
                print("    " + label + " outer=" + str(outer) + " t=" +
                      repr(round(el, 1)) + " score=" + repr(round(float(best_score), 7)))
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 30 == 0 and verbose:
                el = time.monotonic() - t0
                print("    " + label + " stall=" + str(stall) +
                      " t=" + repr(round(el, 1)) + " score=" + repr(round(float(best_score), 7)))

        outer += 1

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a, label, t_global):
    """Evaluate candidate with the internal evaluator and update if better."""
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    sc = score_internal(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_seq = a.copy()
        el = time.monotonic() - t_global
        print("  *** NEW BEST [" + str(label) + "]: " + repr(round(float(sc), 7)) +
              " n=" + str(a.size) + " t=" + repr(round(float(el), 1)) + "s")
    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Main search driver. Returns a Python list of nonnegative finite floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        raw_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        raw_budget = 1800.0
    time_budget = max(120.0, min(raw_budget - 90.0, 1720.0))

    print("Search budget: " + repr(round(time_budget, 1)) + "s")

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # ---------------- Parent / default initialization ----------------
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.sum() > 1e-15:
                pn = normalize_positive(p)
                sc_raw = score_internal(p)
                sc_norm = score_internal(pn)
                if sc_raw <= sc_norm:
                    best_seq = p.copy()
                    best_score = sc_raw
                else:
                    best_seq = pn.copy()
                    best_score = sc_norm
                print("Parent accepted: n=" + str(pn.size) +
                      " score_raw=" + repr(round(float(sc_raw), 7)) +
                      " score_norm=" + repr(round(float(sc_norm), 7)))
    except Exception as e:
        print("No usable parent: " + repr(e))

    if best_seq is None:
        n_def = 6700
        best_score = float("inf")
        cands = initial_candidates(n_def, None)
        for cand in cands:
            sc = score_internal(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = cand.copy()
        if best_seq is None or best_score == float("inf"):
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_def, endpoint=True)
            best_seq = normalize_positive(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 1.5)
            best_score = score_internal(best_seq)
        print("Default init selected: n=" + str(best_seq.size) +
              " score=" + repr(round(float(best_score), 7)))

    # Choose a working length.
    if best_seq.size >= 1500:
        n_main = int(min(9000, max(2500, best_seq.size)))
    else:
        n_main = 6700

    # Keep original best for final comparison; work at n_main for trajectory methods.
    work_seq = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, n_main)
    work_score = score_internal(work_seq)
    if np.isfinite(work_score) and work_score < best_score:
        best_seq = work_seq
        best_score = work_score

    best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, work_seq, "length", t0)

    # ---------------- Early cyclic shift ----------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 120 and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        try:
            shifted, _ = shift_search(best_seq, budget=min(20.0, max(5.0, 0.05 * time_budget)),
                                      n_eval=96, seed=17)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, shifted, "early_shift", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("early_shift failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Low-dimensional smooth search ----------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    low_basis = None
    low_a = None
    low_score = best_score
    if remaining > 280:
        try:
            low_basis = make_basis(n_main)
            low_time = min(320.0, max(60.0, 0.20 * time_budget))
            low_a, low_score = lowdim_search(n_main, low_basis, best_seq, low_time)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, low_a, "lowdim", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("lowdim failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Main mirror descent on current best ----------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 220 and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        t_mirror = min(720.0, max(60.0, 0.40 * time_budget))
        try:
            a_out, _ = mirror_search(best_seq, t_mirror, label="mirror-main",
                                     beta_rel_start=8.0, beta_rel_end=0.02)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "mirror-main", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("mirror-main failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- If lowdim found an interesting basin, polish it --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 300 and low_a is not None and len(low_a) == n_main and low_score < best_score + 0.02:
        t_lowmirror = min(360.0, max(60.0, 0.20 * time_budget))
        try:
            a_out, _ = mirror_search(low_a, t_lowmirror, label="mirror-low",
                                     beta_rel_start=6.0, beta_rel_end=0.03)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "mirror-low", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("mirror-low failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Low-dimensional refinement around new best -------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 180 and low_basis is not None and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        try:
            tld = min(180.0, max(40.0, 0.10 * time_budget))
            if tld > 30.0:
                base_log = make_base_log(normalize_positive(best_seq))
                zero = np.zeros(low_basis.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
                rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
                starts_final = [zero, rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=low_basis.shape[1])]
                beta_final = [3.0, 1.0, 0.3, 0.1]
                maxit_final = 35
                for th in starts_final:
                    if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_budget - 80.0:
                        break
                    _, a_try, sc_try = optimize_lowdim_start(
                        th, low_basis, base_log, beta_final, maxit_final)
                    best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_try,
                                                           "lowdim-final", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("lowdim-final failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Another long mirror on current best --------------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 220 and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        t_mirror2 = min(520.0, max(60.0, 0.30 * time_budget))
        try:
            a_out, _ = mirror_search(best_seq, t_mirror2, label="mirror-2",
                                     beta_rel_start=4.0, beta_rel_end=0.02)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "mirror-2", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("mirror-2 failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Targeted local coordinate moves ------------------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 140 and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        t_local = min(300.0, max(50.0, 0.20 * time_budget))
        try:
            a_out, _ = local_pair_search(best_seq, t_local, label="local-pair")
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "local-pair", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("local-pair failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Final cyclic shift ----------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 70 and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        t_shift_final = min(80.0, max(15.0, 0.08 * time_budget))
        try:
            shifted, _ = shift_search(best_seq, budget=t_shift_final, n_eval=220, seed=999)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, shifted, "final-shift", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("final-shift failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Final mirror micro-polish ----------------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 80 and len(best_seq) == n_main:
        t_final = min(150.0, max(25.0, 0.15 * time_budget))
        try:
            a_out, _ = mirror_search(best_seq, t_final, label="mirror-final",
                                     beta_rel_start=2.0, beta_rel_end=0.015,
                                     eta_start=0.45)
            best_seq, best_score = consider_update(best_seq, best_score, a_out, "mirror-final", t0)
        except Exception as e:
            print("mirror-final failed: " + repr(e))

    # ---------------- Final safe validation ----------------------------------
    if best_seq is None:
        n_fb = 6700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_fb, endpoint=True)
        best_seq = normalize_positive(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 1.5)
        best_score = score_internal(best_seq)

    # Convert to plain float list and sanitize one last time.
    out = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    out[out > 1000.0] = 1000.0
    if out.size == 0 or out.sum() < 0.01:
        n_fb = 6700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_fb, endpoint=True)
        out = normalize_positive(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 1.5)

    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in out]))
        print("FINAL external score: " + repr(round(final_score, 7)) +
              " n=" + str(out.size) +
              " elapsed=" + repr(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
